It is authoritative\n4: with `FULL-GAME-GREENFIELD-REBUILD.md`. Consumers must not invent a second schema\n5: or silently tune these values.\n6: \n7: ## 1. Global Decisions\n8: \n9: | Decision | Frozen value |\n10: |---|---|\n11: | maximum active players | 6; seventh player is rejected with `SERVER_FULL` |\n12: | character loading | `Players.CharacterAutoLoads=false` |\n13: | world streaming | disabled for this bounded slice; substrate/spawns load before characters |\n14: | friendly fire | disabled |\n15: | death | retain profile; one respawn after 8 seconds |\n16: | aggregate carry capacity | none; per-item stack caps only |\n17: | storage capacity | 80 aggregate units + 40 at storage tier 1 |\n18: | persistence schema | `ProfileV1`, new keyspace `FullGameProfiles_v1` |\n19: | upgrades | `harvest`, `weapon`, `storage`; each tier 0..1 |\n20: | old-data import | none; legacy folders, leaderstats, attributes, Tools, and binaries are ignored |\n21: | processor disconnect | reserved Crops are atomically refunded; job is cancelled |\n22: | planted-plot disconnect | one Seed is atomically refunded; plot clears even if mature |\n23: | power at server start | off |\n24: | powered devices | FoodProcessor, three upgrade stations, and switchable main lights |\n25: | backup-powered devices | Shop, Storage, Scrap Exchange, plots, emergency lights |\n26: | rifle magazine/reload/spread | not used; each shot consumes one reserve round and follows the normalized ray |\n27: \n28: ## 2. Literal ProfileV1\n29: \n30: ```text\n31: ProfileV1 = {\n32:   SchemaVersion = 1,\n33:   Revision = 0, -- integer 0..2^31-1\n34:   Inventory = { [ItemId] = integer quantity },\n35:   Storage = { [ItemId] = integer quantity },\n36:   Currencies = { credits = 25 },\n37:   Upgrades = { harvest = 0, weapon = 0, storage = 0 },\n38:   Onboarding = {\n39:     Version = 1,\n40:     Step = \"wake_in_bunker\",\n41:     Completed = false,\n42:   },\n43:   StarterGrantVersions = {},\n44:   Stats = { HighestDay = 0, ZombiesDefeated = 0, TreesHarvested = 0 },\n45: }\n46: ```\n47: \n48: Allowed onboarding steps, in order:\n49: \n50: ```text\n51: wake_in_bunker\n52: harvest_first_wood\n53: plant_first_crop\n54: process_first_food\n55: restore_bunker_power\n56: survive_first_night\n57: onboarding_complete\n58: ```\n59: \n60: Starter transaction key: `starter_v1`. The marker and all starter items mutate\n61: the same profile blob in one transaction. Contents: one `axe_basic`, one\n62: `rifle_basic`, 24 `ammo_rifle`, four `seed_wheat`, and one `battery_cell`.\n63: Default Credits already equal 25.\n64: \n65: Every successful transaction validates all integer bounds and increments\n66: `Revision` exactly once. A rejected transaction changes nothing. Credits are\n67: integers `0..999999`. Upgrade tiers outside `0..1`, unknown keys,\n68: negative/noninteger/nonfinite values, or stack overflows invalidate a record.\n69: Corrupt production records fail closed and never become blank profiles.\n70: \n71: Storage use is the sum of stored quantities. Capacity is\n72: `80 + 40 * storageTier`. Carried inventory uses per-item caps only.\n73: \n74: ## 3. Persistence Protocol\n75: \n76: Production record key: `player:<UserId>` in `FullGameProfiles_v1`.\n77: \n78: ```text\n79: StoredRecord = {\n80:   Profile = ProfileV1,\n81:   Lock = { JobId = string, ExpiresAt = unixSeconds },\n82: }\n83: ```\n84: \n85: - Production uses `DataStoreService:UpdateAsync`; gameplay services never call\n86:   DataStore APIs.\n87: - Studio defaults to an explicit in-memory repository. Production storage needs\n88:   a server-only QA flag.\n89: - Load deadline: 30 seconds, at most five attempts, backoff\n90:   `0.5, 1, 2, 4, 8` seconds plus bounded jitter.\n91: - A nonexpired foreign JobId lock fails with `PROFILE_LOCKED`.\n92: - Lock lease: 120 seconds; renew every 45 seconds during autosave.\n93: - Autosave: 60 seconds with deterministic per-user jitter in `[-5,+5]`.\n94: - Pure migrations run sequentially. V1 has no predecessor import; unknown/newer\n95:   versions fail `PROFILE_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED`.\n96: - Save validates the current JobId and does not overwrite a newer external\n97:   revision (`PROFILE_CONFLICT`).\n98: - Failed production load/save never falls back to defaults. A load failure\n99:   removes the player with a stable retry message.\n100: - `BindToClose` rejects mutations, settles reservations, saves/releases loaded\n101:   profiles in parallel within 25 seconds, then stops services.\n102: \n103: ## 4. Canonical Definitions\n104: \n105: ### Items\n106: \n107: | ItemId | Cap | Runtime behavior |\n108: |---|---:|---|\n109: | `axe_basic` | 1 | hotbar slot 1; server-provisioned Tool |\n110: | `rifle_basic` | 1 | hotbar slot 2; server-provisioned Tool |\n111: | `ammo_rifle` | 240 | one per accepted Rifle shot |\n112: | `wood` | 200 | Tree reward |\n113: | `scrap` | 100 | cache/zombie reward; exchange input |\n114: | `battery_cell` | 10 | bunker power input |\n115: | `seed_wheat` | 50 | plot input |\n116: | `crop_wheat` | 50 | processor input |\n117: | `food_ration` | 20 | heals 35; 3-second cooldown |\n118: \n119: ### Upgrades\n120: \n121: | UpgradeId | Cost | Tier 1 effect | Repeat purchase |\n122: |---|---:|---|---|\n123: | `harvest` | 100 Credits | Tree damage 2 instead of 1 | `ALREADY_OWNED` |\n124: | `weapon` | 200 Credits | Rifle damage 34 instead of 24 | `ALREADY_OWNED` |\n125: | `storage` | 150 Credits | capacity 120 instead of 80 | `ALREADY_OWNED` |\n126: \n127: ### Shop and exchange\n128: \n129: | OfferId | Result | Cost |\n130: |---|---|---:|\n131: | `battery_1` | 1 Battery | 20 Credits |\n132: | `wheat_seed_2` | 2 Seeds | 10 Credits |\n133: | `rifle_ammo_12` | 12 rounds | 15 Credits |\n134: | `ration_1` | 1 Ration | 15 Credits |\n135: \n136: Scrap exchange accepts integer counts 1..20 and atomically removes that many\n137: Scrap while adding `count * 5` Credits. Shop, exchange, storage, upgrades, power,\n138: and starter grants are all-or-nothing. Capacity/currency overflow rejects before\n139: mutation.\n140: \n141: ### Survival tuning\n142: \n143: | Contract | Value |\n144: |---|---|\n145: | Tree health/reward/respawn | 3 / 4 Wood / 60 seconds |\n146: | Tree hit range/cooldown | 10 studs / 0.55 seconds |\n147: | Scrap cache | 2 Scrap, 45-second respawn, 10-stud range |\n148: | Wheat growth/harvest | 45 seconds / 2 Crops + 1 Seed |\n149: | Processor | 2 Crops -> 1 Ration, 8 seconds |\n150: | Rifle | 24/34 damage, 0.28-second cadence, 180-stud range |\n151: | Axe melee | 18 damage, 0.65-second cadence, 8-stud range |\n152: | Basic zombie | 80 health, WalkSpeed 7, 10 damage |\n153: | Zombie melee | 4.5 studs, 1.2-second cooldown |\n154: | Zombie AI | 5 Hz update; path recompute at most once/second/actor |\n155: | Zombie aggro/leash | 220 / 300 studs from spawn |\n156: | Zombie spawn cadence / cap | 1.2 seconds / 18 active |\n157: | day/dusk/night/dawn | 180 / 20 / 150 / 20 seconds |\n158: | power duration | 180 seconds |\n159: \n160: At night start, profile-ready players are sampled once. Budget is\n161: `min(18, 3 + day + 2 * (sampledPlayers - 1))`. Late joiners may become targets\n162: but do not alter that wave or earn its survival credit. A sampled player survives\n163: only if connected and never dead between night start and dawn. Completion means\n164: all budgeted spawns occurred and active wave enemies reached zero, or dawn forced\n165: cleanup.\n166: \n167: Kill credit belongs to the most recent valid damaging player if the hit occurred\n168: within 10 seconds of death. One terminal profile transaction increments\n169: ZombiesDefeated and applies saturating rewards:\n170: `credits=min(999999, credits+4)` and `scrap=min(100, scrap+1)`. The transaction\n171: records the actual granted amounts, completes exactly once even when either cap\n172: is already full, and returns `KILL_REWARD_CAPPED` when either grant was reduced.\n173: It is never retried as a pending world reward.\n174: \n175: On a Tree's potential final hit, capacity is checked first. If all 4 Wood cannot\n176: fit, return `INVENTORY_FULL` and leave health/state unchanged. A final accepted\n177: hit first changes the lifecycle revision to Depleted, then commits one reward.\n178: The same full-reward rule applies to Scrap.\n179: \n180: Wheat harvest requires capacity for the complete `2 crop_wheat + 1 seed_wheat`\n181: reward before mutation. If either item cannot fit, return `INVENTORY_FULL` and\n182: leave the mature plot, owner, and lifecycle revision unchanged.\n183: \n184: ## 5. Service Ownership and APIs\n185: \n186: All implementation modules belong to the single serialized Full-Game\n187: Implementation role. remote connections/buckets/result cache | `Start`, `SendResult`, `Stop` | NetTypes/other services | disconnect/clear |\n209: \n210: Only PlayerData commits profile blobs. Domain services own validation/meaning of\n211: their fields. Replication never mutates gameplay. Interaction validates/routes\n212: and performs no domain mutation itself.\n213: \n214: Frozen domain events:\n215: \n216: ```text\n217: WorldReady, ProfileReady, ProfileChanged, ProfileLeaving,\n218: CharacterSpawned, CharacterDied, ItemAdded, StorageChanged,\n219: EquipmentChanged, ToolHit, TreeHarvested, ScrapCollected, ResourceChanged,\n220: PlotPlanted, CropHarvested, FoodProcessed, FarmChanged,\n221: PurchaseCompleted, UpgradePurchased, PowerActivated, PowerExpired,\n222: PlayerDied, ZombieKilled, WaveChanged, NightSurvived,\n223: PhaseChanged, OnboardingChanged, InteractionOpened\n224: ```\n225: \n226: Every service implements idempotent `Init`, `Start`, and `Stop`. Bootstrap starts\n227: in table order and stops in reverse. Failed Init/Start stops prior services and\n228: loads no character.\n229: \n230: ## 6. Typed Network Contract\n231: \n232: All payloads reject missing/extra keys, metatables, nesting deeper than two,\n233: nonprintable strings, and invalid Instances.\n234: \n235: - `RequestId`: ASCII `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`, length 1..36.\n236: - canonical IDs: printable ASCII, length 1..48, exact registry match.\n237: - station token: 32 lowercase hexadecimal characters, server-generated.\n238: - counts: integer 1..50 unless narrower below.\n239: - aim direction: finite Vector3, magnitude `0.99..1.01`.\n240: - no client origin is accepted; server character/tool supplies it.\n241: \n242: Source-authored remotes:\n243: \n244: ```text\n245: GetSnapshot: RemoteFunction, C->S, exactly zero arguments\n246: StateDelta: RemoteEvent, S->C\n247: ActionResult: RemoteEvent, S->C\n248: InteractionOpened: RemoteEvent, S->C\n249: ToolIntent: RemoteEvent, C->S\n250: InteractionIntent: RemoteEvent, C->S\n251: InteractionCloseIntent: RemoteEvent, C->S\n252: UseItemIntent: RemoteEvent, C->S\n253: ```\n254: \n255: Exact unions:\n256: \n257: ```text\n258: ToolIntent:\n259:   {RequestId, Kind=\"Equip\", Slot=1|2}\n260:   {RequestId, Kind=\"Primary\", AimDirection=Vector3}\n261: \n262: UseItemIntent:\n263:   {RequestId, Kind=\"UseItem\", ItemId=\"food_ration\"}\n264: \n265: InteractionIntent:\n266:   {RequestId, Kind=\"FarmPlant\", InteractionId, Token, CropId=\"wheat\"}\n267:   {RequestId, Kind=\"FarmHarvest\", InteractionId, Token}\n268:   {RequestId, Kind=\"ProcessorStart\", InteractionId, Token, RecipeId=\"food_ration_wheat\"}\n269:   {RequestId, Kind=\"ShopBuy\", InteractionId, Token, OfferId, Count=1..5}\n270:   {RequestId, Kind=\"StorageTransfer\", InteractionId, Token, Direction=\"deposit\"|\"withdraw\", ItemId, Count=1..50}\n271:   {RequestId, Kind=\"PowerActivate\", InteractionId, Token}\n272:   {RequestId, Kind=\"ScrapExchange\", InteractionId, Token, Count=1..20}\n273:   {RequestId, Kind=\"UpgradeBuy\", InteractionId, Token, UpgradeId=\"harvest\"|\"weapon\"|\"storage\"}\n274: \n275: InteractionCloseIntent:\n276:   {Token}\n277: ```\n278: \n279: ProximityPrompts only request a session. Server `PromptTriggered` validates\n280: player/living state/range/line of sight and sends `InteractionOpened`; it never\n281: mutates a domain. The token plus `InteractionIntent` is the sole station mutation\n282: ingress. Tokens expire after 10 seconds and invalidate on death/removal/panel\n283: close/target loss/distance over 12 studs. Every client-side panel close emits\n284: `InteractionCloseIntent`; the server clears only a session still owned by that\n285: player and carrying that exact token, so a delayed close cannot revoke a newer\n286: session.\n287: \n288: Concrete replicated types:\n289: \n290: ```text\n291: ItemQuantities = {\n292:   axe_basic=integer, rifle_basic=integer, ammo_rifle=integer,\n293:   wood=integer, scrap=integer, battery_cell=integer,\n294:   seed_wheat=integer, crop_wheat=integer, food_ration=integer,\n295: }\n296: \n297: PlayerView = {\n298:   ProfileRevision=integer,\n299:   Inventory=ItemQuantities,\n300:   Storage=ItemQuantities, -- axe_basic/rifle_basic are always 0; not storable\n301:   StorageUsed=integer, StorageCapacity=80|120, Credits=integer,\n302:   Upgrades={harvest=0|1, weapon=0|1, storage=0|1},\n303:   Onboarding={Version=1, Step=OnboardingStep, Completed=boolean},\n304:   Stats={HighestDay=integer, ZombiesDefeated=integer, TreesHarvested=integer},\n305:   Health=number, MaxHealth=number, EquippedSlot=0|1|2,\n306:   Hotbar={\n307:     {Slot=1, ItemId=\"axe_basic\", Owned=boolean, Equipped=boolean},\n308:     {Slot=2, ItemId=\"rifle_basic\", Owned=boolean, Equipped=boolean},\n309:   },\n310:   SpawnProtectionEndsAt=number,\n311: }\n312: \n313: WaveView = {\n314:   State=\"Idle\"|\"Spawning\"|\"Active\"|\"Complete\",\n315:   Revision=integer, Budget=integer, Spawned=integer, Alive=integer,\n316:   EligiblePlayers=integer,\n317: }\n318: \n319: PowerView = {IsActive=boolean, Generation=integer, EndsAt=number}\n320: \n321: SessionView = {\n322:   Day=integer, Phase=\"Day\"|\"Dusk\"|\"Night\"|\"Dawn\",\n323:   PhaseRevision=integer, PhaseEndsAt=number,\n324:   Wave=WaveView, Power=PowerView,\n325: }\n326: \n327: PlotView = {\n328:   PlotId=string, State=\"Empty\"|\"Growing\"|\"Mature\",\n329:   IsOwned=boolean, IsOwnedByYou=boolean,\n330:   CropId=nil|\"wheat\", ReadyAt=number,\n331: }\n332: \n333: ProcessorJobView = nil | {\n334:   State=\"Processing\", RecipeId=\"food_ration_wheat\", CompletesAt=number,\n335: }\n336: \n337: WorldView = {Plots={PlotView -- exactly 12, sorted by PlotId}, ProcessorJob=ProcessorJobView}\n338: \n339: Snapshot = {\n340:   Protocol=1, ViewRevision=integer, ServerTime=number,\n341:   Player=PlayerView, Session=SessionView, World=WorldView,\n342: }\n343: \n344: GetSnapshotResponse =\n345:   {Success=true, Code=\"OK\", Snapshot=Snapshot}\n346:   | {Success=false,\n347:      Code=\"PROFILE_NOT_READY\"|\"PROFILE_LOAD_FAILED\"|\"RATE_LIMITED\",\n348:      RetryAfter=number} -- finite 0.5..5\n349: ```\n350: \n351: `InteractionOpened` exact payload:\n352: \n353: ```text\n354: {\n355:   InteractionId=string,\n356:   InteractionKind=\"farm_plot\"|\"food_processor\"|\"shop\"|\"storage\"|\n357:     \"power\"|\"scrap_exchange\"|\"upgrade\",\n358:   Token=string, ExpiresAt=number,\n359:   Actions={string -- exact allowed actions, sorted},\n360:   View=InteractionView,\n361: }\n362: \n363: InteractionView =\n364:   {Kind=\"farm_plot\", Plot=PlotView, SeedQuantity=integer}\n365:   | {Kind=\"food_processor\", IsPowered=boolean,\n366:      CropQuantity=integer, Job=ProcessorJobView}\n367: IsPowered=boolean}\n376: ```\n377: \n378: `Actions` values are limited to `FarmPlant`, `FarmHarvest`, `ProcessorStart`,\n379: `ShopBuy`, `StorageTransfer`, `PowerActivate`, `ScrapExchange`, and `UpgradeBuy`.\n380: Offer arrays are sorted by OfferId and contain the four frozen offers only.\n381: \n382: Successful `ActionResult.Payload` is one of these exact operation payloads;\n383: failed results have `Payload=nil`:\n384: \n385: ```text\n386: {Kind=\"Equip\", EquippedSlot=1|2}\n387: {Kind=\"Primary\", HitKind=\"None\"|\"Tree\"|\"Zombie\",\n388:  TargetId=nil|string, TargetHealth=nil|number, AmmoRemaining=integer}\n389: {Kind=\"UseItem\", ItemId=\"food_ration\", Quantity=integer,\n390:  Health=number, MaxHealth=number}\n391: {Kind=\"FarmPlant\", PlotId=string, State=\"Growing\", ReadyAt=number}\n392: {Kind=\"FarmHarvest\", PlotId=string, State=\"Empty\",\n393:  CropGranted=2, SeedGranted=1}\n394: {Kind=\"ProcessorStart\", RecipeId=\"food_ration_wheat\", CompletesAt=number}\n395: {Kind=\"ShopBuy\", OfferId=string, Count=integer, Credits=integer,\n396:  GrantedItemId=string, GrantedQuantity=integer}\n397: {Kind=\"StorageTransfer\", Direction=\"deposit\"|\"withdraw\", ItemId=string,\n398:  Count=integer, StorageUsed=integer, StorageCapacity=80|120}\n399: {Kind=\"PowerActivate\", EndsAt=number, BatteryQuantity=integer}\n400: {Kind=\"ScrapExchange\", Count=integer, Credits=integer, ScrapQuantity=integer}\n401: {Kind=\"UpgradeBuy\", UpgradeId=\"harvest\"|\"weapon\"|\"storage\",\n402:  Tier=1, Credits=integer}\n403: ```\n404: \n405: Every `ActionResult` has exact base shape\n406: `{RequestId, Success, Code, Revision, Payload}` where Revision is the current\n407: ViewRevision. A duplicate whose result remains in cache returns that exact prior\n408: result without rate consumption or mutation. Cache: up to 64 results/player for\n409: 30 seconds. If an observed RequestId remains inside that 30-second replay window\n410: after its result has been evicted from the bounded cache, it returns failed\n411: `REPLAYED`; it never re-dispatches, mutates a gameplay domain, or reinserts the\n412: evicted result.\n413: \n414: Delta is exactly one of:\n415: \n416: ```text\n417: {Protocol=1, BaseRevision, Revision, ServerTime, Domain=\"Player\", Value=PlayerView}\n418: {Protocol=1, BaseRevision, Revision, ServerTime, Domain=\"Session\", Value=SessionView}\n419: {Protocol=1, BaseRevision, Revision, ServerTime, Domain=\"World\", Value=WorldView}\n420: ```\n421: \n422: Revision mismatch makes the client discard the delta and request a snapshot.\n423: Countdown is interpolated from server timestamps; no per-second remote.\n424: \n425: | Channel | Burst | Refill/sec |\n426: |---|---:|---:|\n427: | GetSnapshot | 2 | 0.5 |\n428: | ToolIntent | 12 | 6 |\n429: | InteractionIntent | 6 | 3 |\n430: | InteractionCloseIntent | 6 | 3 |\n431: | UseItemIntent | 3 | 1 |\n432: \n433: Gameplay cooldowns also apply. The server derives rays, validates equipped\n434: runtime ownership/range/LOS, and owns zombie assemblies. Clients never send\n435: damage, origin, reward, price, growth, health, phase, or power values.\n436: \n437: Stable result codes:\n438: \n439: ```text\n440: OK, INVALID_PAYLOAD, UNKNOWN_REQUEST, RATE_LIMITED, REPLAYED,\n441: PROFILE_NOT_READY, PROFILE_LOCKED, PROFILE_LOAD_FAILED,\n442: PROFILE_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED, PROFILE_CONFLICT, SERVER_FULL,\n443: PLAYER_DEAD, NO_CHARACTER, INVALID_SLOT, NOT_EQUIPPED, COOLDOWN,\n444: NO_AMMO, INVALID_AIM, OUT_OF_RANGE, LINE_OF_SIGHT_BLOCKED,\n445: UNKNOWN_TARGET, TARGET_INACTIVE, INVENTORY_FULL, NOT_ENOUGH_ITEMS,\n446: NOT_ENOUGH_CREDITS, STORAGE_FULL, ALREADY_OWNED, REQUIRES_POWER,\n447: INVALID_TOKEN, TOKEN_EXPIRED, WRONG_INTERACTION, PLOT_OCCUPIED,\n448: PLOT_NOT_READY, NOT_PLOT_OWNER, PROCESSOR_BUSY, POWER_ALREADY_ACTIVE,\n449: KILL_REWARD_CAPPED\n450: ```\n451: \n452: ## 7. Spawn and Player Lifecycle\n453: \n454: All spawns are `(5,1,5)`, invisible, anchored, noncolliding, neutral, Enabled,\n455: Duration 0, and face the bedroom exit.\n456: \n457: | SpawnId | Slot | Position |\n458: |---|---:|---:|\n459: | `bedroom_01` | 1 | `(-49,54.5,72)` |\n460: | `bedroom_02` | 2 | `(-41,54.5,72)` |\n461: | `bedroom_03` | 3 | `(-33,54.5,72)` |\n462: | `bedroom_04` | 4 | `(-49,54.5,62)` |\n463: | `bedroom_05` | 5 | `(-41,54.5,62)` |\n464: | `bedroom_06` | 6 | `(-33,54.5,62)` |\n465: \n466: Smallest-free slot is held for the session and reused on respawn. Join order:\n467: \n468: `validate world -> enforce cap -> load profile -> reserve slot -> set RespawnLocation -> commit starter_v1 -> LoadCharacterAsync -> provision equipment -> publish snapshot`\n469: \n470: Death increments respawn generation, invalidates interactions/equipment, records\n471: night death, then schedules one reload in 8 seconds only if the same session and\n472: generation remain active.\n473: \n474: The map is nonstreamed. Spawn-safe volume is\n475: `X=-62..-25, Y=52..70, Z=50..82`; characters receive 10 seconds of server-owned\n476: spawn protection and zombies ignore protected characters.\n477: \n478: Player teardown:\n479: \n480: 1. mark Leaving and reject intents;\n481: 2. invalidate tokens/request cache/rate buckets;\n482: 3. cancel/refund processor and plot reservations;\n483: 4. cancel respawn generation and release slot;\n484: 5. destroy Tools and unregister combat actor;\n485: 6. remove wave eligibility and replication cache;\n486: 7. save profile, release lease, clear profile.\n487: \n488: ## 8. Lifecycle State Machines\n574: \n575: Every delayed callback captures a lifecycle generation and returns if stale.\n576: \n577: | Trigger | Required transition / cleanup |\n578: |---|---|\n579: | startup failure | reject joins; reverse-stop; destroy Runtime; load no characters |\n580: | character death | invalidate tokens/tools; unregister actor; mark night death; one generation-checked respawn |\n581: | respawn | same slot; register actor; reprovision Tools; republish Player view |\n582: | player removal | exact seven-step teardown in section 7 |\n583: | dawn | stop spawns; invalidate wave; destroy enemies; settle survivors; enemy count zero |\n584: | wave completion | publish Complete; clear actor/path/connections; no second wave that night |\n585: | power expiry | compare generation; disable exact powered set; publish once |\n586: | node respawn | compare cycle; restore health/visibility/prompt once |\n587: | processor finish | compare job; commit reserved output; clear once |\n588: | BindToClose | reject requests; settle/refund; reverse-stop; save/release within 25 seconds |\n589: \n590: Processor output capacity is reserved at job start, so successful start guarantees\n591: completion; otherwise it rejects before removing Crops.\n592: \n593: ## 10. Hard Performance and QA Gates\n621: \n622: - GameWorld <= 2,500 BaseParts / 3,600 descendants.\n623: - Bunker <= 600 BaseParts / 900 descendants.\n624: - Farm <= 180 BaseParts / 40 collidable parts.\n625: - Lights <= 23, shadow lights <= 6.\n626: - ParticleEmitters <= 12, configured visible budget <= 180.\n627: - Active Sounds <= 16, ambient beds <= 2.\n628: - Active zombies <= 18, path recomputations <= 18/second.\n629: \n630: Target: current Apple M1 Pro Studio, 1920x1080, quality 5. During a sustained\n631: 10-minute six-client/full-wave test after two-minute warmup:\n632: \n633: - client frame p95 <= 33.3 ms;\n634: - server heartbeat/script p95 <= 16.7 ms and mean <= 8 ms;\n635: - steady replication receive <= 20 KB/s/client; burst <= 75 KB/s/client;\n636: - memory growth after warmup <= 25 MB;\n637: - after dawn and all players leave, Runtime descendants, service actor maps,\n638:   tokens, buckets, jobs, and tracked connections return exactly to baseline.\n639: \n640: UI checks also cover `390x844`, `1024x768`, and `1280x720` gamepad layouts.\n641: \n642: Play matrix:\n643: \n644: - one player: full loop, death/respawn, rejoin;\n645: - two players: simultaneous Tree final hit, plot, processor, power, purchase,\n646:   storage, attribution, and leave during each reservation;\n647: - six players: all slots, full wave, joins/leaves, UI, cleanup; seventh rejection;\n648: - remote fuzz: every missing/extra/wrong type, nonfinite vector, oversized value,\n649:   stale/foreign token, replay, spam, dead/not-ready, obstructed/out-of-range/\n650:   unequipped target; zero unauthorized mutation;\n651: - persistence: first join/rejoin, starter marker, lock conflict, migration harness,\n652:   load/save failure, revision conflict, autosave, shutdown.\n653: \n654: Static Gate B uses an explicit cutover file manifest because Git does not track\n655: this subtree. It fails whitespace errors, validates every changed Lua/Luau AST\n656: and JSON file, generates the builder twice hash-identically, and runs canonical\n657: Rojo 7.7.0 sourcemap and build.\n\n(End of file - total 657 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"# FG-GREENFIELD-001 Frozen Technical Contracts\n\nThis document supplies the executable details for Gate A. It is authoritative\nwith `FULL-GAME-GREENFIELD-REBUILD.md`. Consumers must not invent a second schema\nor silently tune these values.\n\n## 1. Global Decisions\n\n| Decision | Frozen value |\n|---|---|\n| maximum active players | 6; seventh player is rejected with `SERVER_FULL` |\n| character loading | `Players.CharacterAutoLoads=false` |\n| world streaming | disabled for this bounded slice; substrate/spawns load before characters |\n| friendly fire | disabled |\n| death | retain profile; one respawn after 8 seconds |\n| aggregate carry capacity | none; per-item stack caps only |\n| storage capacity | 80 aggregate units + 40 at storage tier 1 |\n| persistence schema | `ProfileV1`, new keyspace `FullGameProfiles_v1` |\n| upgrades | `harvest`, `weapon`, `storage`; each tier 0..1 |\n| old-data import | none; legacy folders, leaderstats, attributes, Tools, and binaries are ignored |\n| processor disconnect | reserved Crops are atomically refunded; job is cancelled |\n| planted-plot disconnect | one Seed is atomically refunded; plot clears even if mature |\n| power at server start | off |\n| powered devices | FoodProcessor, three upgrade stations, and switchable main lights |\n| backup-powered devices | Shop, Storage, Scrap Exchange, plots, emergency lights |\n| rifle magazine/reload/spread | not used; each shot consumes one reserve round and follows the normalized ray |\n\n## 2. Literal ProfileV1\n\n```text\nProfileV1 = {\n  SchemaVersion = 1,\n  Revision = 0, -- integer 0..2^31-1\n  Inventory = { [ItemId] = integer quantity },\n  Storage = { [ItemId] = integer quantity },\n  Currencies = { credits = 25 },\n  Upgrades = { harvest = 0, weapon = 0, storage = 0 },\n  Onboarding = {\n    Version = 1,\n    Step = \"wake_in_bunker\",\n    Completed = false,\n  },\n  StarterGrantVersions = {},\n  Stats = { HighestDay = 0, ZombiesDefeated = 0, TreesHarvested = 0 },\n}\n```\n\nAllowed onboarding steps, in order:\n\n```text\nwake_in_bunker\nharvest_first_wood\nplant_first_crop\nprocess_first_food\nrestore_bunker_power\nsurvive_first_night\nonboarding_complete\n```\n\nStarter transaction key: `starter_v1`. The marker and all starter items mutate\nthe same profile blob in one transaction. Contents: one `axe_basic`, one\n`rifle_basic`, 24 `ammo_rifle`, four `seed_wheat`, and one `battery_cell`.\nDefault Credits already equal 25.\n\nEvery successful transaction validates all integer bounds and increments\n`Revision` exactly once. A rejected transaction changes nothing. Credits are\nintegers `0..999999`. Upgrade tiers outside `0..1`, unknown keys,\nnegative/noninteger/nonfinite values, or stack overflows invalidate a record.\nCorrupt production records fail closed and never become blank profiles.\n\nStorage use is the sum of stored quantities. Capacity is\n`80 + 40 * storageTier`. Carried inventory uses per-item caps only.\n\n## 3. Persistence Protocol\n\nProduction record key: `player:<UserId>` in `FullGameProfiles_v1`.\n\n```text\nStoredRecord = {\n  Profile = ProfileV1,\n  Lock = { JobId = string, ExpiresAt = unixSeconds },\n}\n```\n\n- Production uses `DataStoreService:UpdateAsync`; gameplay services never call\n  DataStore APIs.\n- Studio defaults to an explicit in-memory repository. Production storage needs\n  a server-only QA flag.\n- Load deadline: 30 seconds, at most five attempts, backoff\n  `0.5, 1, 2, 4, 8` seconds plus bounded jitter.\n- A nonexpired foreign JobId lock fails with `PROFILE_LOCKED`.\n- Lock lease: 120 seconds; renew every 45 seconds during autosave.\n- Autosave: 60 seconds with deterministic per-user jitter in `[-5,+5]`.\n- Pure migrations run sequentially. V1 has no predecessor import; unknown/newer\n  versions fail `PROFILE_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED`.\n- Save validates the current JobId and does not overwrite a newer external\n  revision (`PROFILE_CONFLICT`).\n- Failed production load/save never falls back to defaults. A load failure\n  removes the player with a stable retry message.\n- `BindToClose` rejects mutations, settles reservations, saves/releases loaded\n  profiles in parallel within 25 seconds, then stops services.\n\n## 4. Canonical Definitions\n\n### Items\n\n| ItemId | Cap | Runtime behavior |\n|---|---:|---|\n| `axe_basic` | 1 | hotbar slot 1; server-provisioned Tool |\n| `rifle_basic` | 1 | hotbar slot 2; server-provisioned Tool |\n| `ammo_rifle` | 240 | one per accepted Rifle shot |\n| `wood` | 200 | Tree reward |\n| `scrap` | 100 | cache/zombie reward; exchange input |\n| `battery_cell` | 10 | bunker power input |\n| `seed_wheat` | 50 | plot input |\n| `crop_wheat` | 50 | processor input |\n| `food_ration` | 20 | heals 35; 3-second cooldown |\n\n### Upgrades\n\n| UpgradeId | Cost | Tier 1 effect | Repeat purchase |\n|---|---:|---|---|\n| `harvest` | 100 Credits | Tree damage 2 instead of 1 | `ALREADY_OWNED` |\n| `weapon` | 200 Credits | Rifle damage 34 instead of 24 | `ALREADY_OWNED` |\n| `storage` | 150 Credits | capacity 120 instead of 80 | `ALREADY_OWNED` |\n\n### Shop and exchange\n\n| OfferId | Result | Cost |\n|---|---|---:|\n| `battery_1` | 1 Battery | 20 Credits |\n| `wheat_seed_2` | 2 Seeds | 10 Credits |\n| `rifle_ammo_12` | 12 rounds | 15 Credits |\n| `ration_1` | 1 Ration | 15 Credits |\n\nScrap exchange accepts integer counts 1..20 and atomically removes that many\nScrap while adding `count * 5` Credits. Shop, exchange, storage, upgrades, power,\nand starter grants are all-or-nothing. Capacity/currency overflow rejects before\nmutation.\n\n### Survival tuning\n\n| Contract | Value |\n|---|---|\n| Tree health/reward/respawn | 3 / 4 Wood / 60 seconds |\n| Tree hit range/cooldown | 10 studs / 0.55 seconds |\n| Scrap cache | 2 Scrap, 45-second respawn, 10-stud range |\n| Wheat growth/harvest | 45 seconds / 2 Crops + 1 Seed |\n| Processor | 2 Crops -> 1 Ration, 8 seconds |\n| Rifle | 24/34 damage, 0.28-second cadence, 180-stud range |\n| Axe melee | 18 damage, 0.65-second cadence, 8-stud range |\n| Basic zombie | 80 health, WalkSpeed 7, 10 damage |\n| Zombie melee | 4.5 studs, 1.2-second cooldown |\n| Zombie AI | 5 Hz update; path recompute at most once/second/actor |\n| Zombie aggro/leash | 220 / 300 studs from spawn |\n| Zombie spawn cadence / cap | 1.2 seconds / 18 active |\n| day/dusk/night/dawn | 180 / 20 / 150 / 20 seconds |\n| power duration | 180 seconds |\n\nAt night start, profile-ready players are sampled once. Budget is\n`min(18, 3 + day + 2 * (sampledPlayers - 1))`. Late joiners may become targets\nbut do not alter that wave or earn its survival credit. A sampled player survives\nonly if connected and never dead between night start and dawn. Completion means\nall budgeted spawns occurred and active wave enemies reached zero, or dawn forced\ncleanup.\n\nKill credit belongs to the most recent valid damaging player if the hit occurred\nwithin 10 seconds of death. One terminal profile transaction increments\nZombiesDefeated and applies saturating rewards:\n`credits=min(999999, credits+4)` and `scrap=min(100, scrap+1)`. The transaction\nrecords the actual granted amounts, completes exactly once even when either cap\nis already full, and returns `KILL_REWARD_CAPPED` when either grant was reduced.\nIt is never retried as a pending world reward.\n\nOn a Tree's potential final hit, capacity is checked first. If all 4 Wood cannot\nfit, return `INVENTORY_FULL` and leave health/state unchanged. A final accepted\nhit first changes the lifecycle revision to Depleted, then commits one reward.\nThe same full-reward rule applies to Scrap.\n\nWheat harvest requires capacity for the complete `2 crop_wheat + 1 seed_wheat`\nreward before mutation. If either item cannot fit, return `INVENTORY_FULL` and\nleave the mature plot, owner, and lifecycle revision unchanged.\n\n## 5. Service Ownership and APIs\n\nAll implementation modules belong to the single serialized Full-Game\nImplementation role. Independent reviewers do not edit implementation.\n\n| Module | Sole mutable state | Public API / callers | Dependencies | Cleanup |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `WorldService` | validated world index | `ClearCharacter` | Inventory/Combat/Resources | destroy Tools/maps |\n| `ResourceService` | node health/cycle/respawn generation | `TryToolHit`, `TryCollectScrap`, `GetView` | World/Inventory | invalidate/reset |\n| `FarmingService` | plots/growth/processor jobs | `Plant`, `Harvest`, `StartProcessor`, `GetView`, `ReleasePlayer` | World/Inventory/Bunker | refund/clear |\n| `EconomyService` | `TimeService` | Day/Phase/PhaseEndsAt/revision | `StartClock`, `GetView`, guarded `SetStudioTimeScale` | Events | invalidate clock |\n| `OnboardingService` | onboarding transitions inside profile transactions | named event consumers, `GetView` | PlayerData/Events | disconnect |\n| `NetworkService` | remote connections/buckets/result cache | `Start`, `SendResult`, `Stop` | NetTypes/other services | disconnect/clear |\n\nOnly PlayerData commits profile blobs. Domain services own validation/meaning of\ntheir fields. Replication never mutates gameplay. Interaction validates/routes\nand performs no domain mutation itself.\n\nFrozen domain events:\n\n```text\nWorldReady, ProfileReady, ProfileChanged, ProfileLeaving,\nCharacterSpawned, CharacterDied, ItemAdded, StorageChanged,\nEquipmentChanged, ToolHit, TreeHarvested, ScrapCollected, ResourceChanged,\nPlotPlanted, CropHarvested, FoodProcessed, FarmChanged,\nPurchaseCompleted, UpgradePurchased, PowerActivated, PowerExpired,\nPlayerDied, ZombieKilled, WaveChanged, NightSurvived,\nPhaseChanged, OnboardingChanged, InteractionOpened\n```\n\nEvery service implements idempotent `Init`, `Start`, and `Stop`. Bootstrap starts\nin table order and stops in reverse. Failed Init/Start stops prior services and\nloads no character.\n\n## 6. Typed Network Contract\n\nAll payloads reject missing/extra keys, metatables, nesting deeper than two,\nnonprintable strings, and invalid Instances.\n\n- `RequestId`: ASCII `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`, length 1..36.\n- canonical IDs: printable ASCII, length 1..48, exact registry match.\n- station token: 32 lowercase hexadecimal characters, server-generated.\n- counts: integer 1..50 unless narrower below.\n- aim direction: finite Vector3, magnitude `0.99..1.01`.\n- no client origin is accepted; server character/tool supplies it.\n\nSource-authored remotes:\n\n```text\nGetSnapshot: RemoteFunction, C->S, exactly zero arguments\nStateDelta: RemoteEvent, S->C\nActionResult: RemoteEvent, S->C\nInteractionOpened: RemoteEvent, S->C\nToolIntent: RemoteEvent, C->S\nInteractionIntent: RemoteEvent, C->S\nInteractionCloseIntent: RemoteEvent, C->S\nUseItemIntent: RemoteEvent, C->S\n```\n\nExact unions:\n\n```text\nToolIntent:\n  {RequestId, Kind=\"Equip\", Slot=1|2}\n  {RequestId, Kind=\"Primary\", AimDirection=Vector3}\n\nUseItemIntent:\n  {RequestId, Kind=\"UseItem\", ItemId=\"food_ration\"}\n\nInteractionIntent:\n  {RequestId, Kind=\"FarmPlant\", InteractionId, Token, CropId=\"wheat\"}\n  {RequestId, Kind=\"FarmHarvest\", InteractionId, Token}\n  {RequestId, Kind=\"ProcessorStart\", InteractionId, Token, RecipeId=\"food_ration_wheat\"}\n  {RequestId, Kind=\"ShopBuy\", InteractionId, Token, OfferId, Count=1..5}\n  {RequestId, Kind=\"StorageTransfer\", InteractionId, Token, Direction=\"deposit\"|\"withdraw\", ItemId, Count=1..50}\n  {RequestId, Kind=\"PowerActivate\", InteractionId, Token}\n  {RequestId, Kind=\"ScrapExchange\", InteractionId, Token, Count=1..20}\n  {RequestId, Kind=\"UpgradeBuy\", InteractionId, Token, UpgradeId=\"harvest\"|\"weapon\"|\"storage\"}\n\nInteractionCloseIntent:\n  {Token}\n```\n\nProximityPrompts only request a session. Server `PromptTriggered` validates\nplayer/living state/range/line of sight and sends `InteractionOpened`; it never\nmutates a domain. The token plus `InteractionIntent` is the sole station mutation\ningress. Tokens expire after 10 seconds and invalidate on death/removal/panel\nclose/target loss/distance over 12 studs. Every client-side panel close emits\n`InteractionCloseIntent`; the server clears only a session still owned by that\nplayer and carrying that exact token, so a delayed close cannot revoke a newer\nsession.\n\nConcrete replicated types:\n\n```text\nItemQuantities = {\n  axe_basic=integer, rifle_basic=integer, ammo_rifle=integer,\n  wood=integer, scrap=integer, battery_cell=integer,\n  seed_wheat=integer, crop_wheat=integer, food_ration=integer,\n}\n\nPlayerView = {\n  ProfileRevision=integer,\n  Inventory=ItemQuantities,\n  Storage=ItemQuantities, -- axe_basic/rifle_basic are always 0; not storable\n  StorageUsed=integer, StorageCapacity=80|120, Credits=integer,\n  Upgrades={harvest=0|1, weapon=0|1, storage=0|1},\n  Onboarding={Version=1, Step=OnboardingStep, Completed=boolean},\n  Stats={HighestDay=integer, ZombiesDefeated=integer, TreesHarvested=integer},\n  Health=number, MaxHealth=number, EquippedSlot=0|1|2,\n  Hotbar={\n    {Slot=1, ItemId=\"axe_basic\", Owned=boolean, Equipped=boolean},\n    {Slot=2, ItemId=\"rifle_basic\", Owned=boolean, Equipped=boolean},\n  },\n  SpawnProtectionEndsAt=number,\n}\n\nWaveView = {\n  State=\"Idle\"|\"Spawning\"|\"Active\"|\"Complete\",\n  Revision=integer, Budget=integer, Spawned=integer, Alive=integer,\n  EligiblePlayers=integer,\n}\n\nPowerView = {IsActive=boolean, Generation=integer, EndsAt=number}\n\nSessionView = {\n  Day=integer, Phase=\"Day\"|\"Dusk\"|\"Night\"|\"Dawn\",\n  PhaseRevision=integer, PhaseEndsAt=number,\n  Wave=WaveView, Power=PowerView,\n}\n\nPlotView = {\n  PlotId=string, State=\"Empty\"|\"Growing\"|\"Mature\",\n  IsOwned=boolean, IsOwnedByYou=boolean,\n  CropId=nil|\"wheat\", ReadyAt=number,\n}\n\nProcessorJobView = nil | {\n  State=\"Processing\", RecipeId=\"food_ration_wheat\", CompletesAt=number,\n}\n\nWorldView = {Plots={PlotView -- exactly 12, sorted by PlotId}, ProcessorJob=ProcessorJobView}\n\nSnapshot = {\n  Protocol=1, ViewRevision=integer, ServerTime=number,\n  Player=PlayerView, Session=SessionView, World=WorldView,\n}\n\nGetSnapshotResponse =\n  {Success=true, Code=\"OK\", Snapshot=Snapshot}\n  | {Success=false,\n     Code=\"PROFILE_NOT_READY\"|\"PROFILE_LOAD_FAILED\"|\"RATE_LIMITED\",\n     RetryAfter=number} -- finite 0.5..5\n```\n\n`InteractionOpened` exact payload:\n\n```text\n{\n  InteractionId=string,\n  InteractionKind=\"farm_plot\"|\"food_processor\"|\"shop\"|\"storage\"|\n    \"power\"|\"scrap_exchange\"|\"upgrade\",\n  Token=string, ExpiresAt=number,\n  Actions={string -- exact allowed actions, sorted},\n  View=InteractionView,\n}\n\nInteractionView =\n  {Kind=\"farm_plot\", Plot=PlotView, SeedQuantity=integer}\n  | {Kind=\"food_processor\", IsPowered=boolean,\n     CropQuantity=integer, Job=ProcessorJobView}\n  | {Kind=\"shop\", Credits=integer,\n     Offers={{OfferId=string, BundleCount=integer, CreditCost=integer}}}\n  | {Kind=\"storage\", Inventory=ItemQuantities, Storage=ItemQuantities,\n     StorageUsed=integer, StorageCapacity=80|120}\n  | {Kind=\"power\", IsActive=boolean, EndsAt=number, BatteryQuantity=integer}\n  | {Kind=\"scrap_exchange\", ScrapQuantity=integer, Credits=integer,\n     CreditsPerScrap=5}\n  | {Kind=\"upgrade\", UpgradeId=\"harvest\"|\"weapon\"|\"storage\",\n     Tier=0|1, CreditCost=integer, Credits=integer, IsPowered=boolean}\n```\n\n`Actions` values are limited to `FarmPlant`, `FarmHarvest`, `ProcessorStart`,\n`ShopBuy`, `StorageTransfer`, `PowerActivate`, `ScrapExchange`, and `UpgradeBuy`.\nOffer arrays are sorted by OfferId and contain the four frozen offers only.\n\nSuccessful `ActionResult.Payload` is one of these exact operation payloads;\nfailed results have `Payload=nil`:\n\n```text\n{Kind=\"Equip\", EquippedSlot=1|2}\n{Kind=\"Primary\", HitKind=\"None\"|\"Tree\"|\"Zombie\",\n TargetId=nil|string, TargetHealth=nil|number, AmmoRemaining=integer}\n{Kind=\"UseItem\", ItemId=\"food_ration\", Quantity=integer,\n Health=number, MaxHealth=number}\n{Kind=\"FarmPlant\", PlotId=string, State=\"Growing\", ReadyAt=number}\n{Kind=\"FarmHarvest\", PlotId=string, State=\"Empty\",\n CropGranted=2, SeedGranted=1}\n{Kind=\"ProcessorStart\", RecipeId=\"food_ration_wheat\", CompletesAt=number}\n{Kind=\"ShopBuy\", OfferId=string, Count=integer, Credits=integer,\n GrantedItemId=string, GrantedQuantity=integer}\n{Kind=\"StorageTransfer\", Direction=\"deposit\"|\"withdraw\", ItemId=string,\n Count=integer, StorageUsed=integer, StorageCapacity=80|120}\n{Kind=\"PowerActivate\", EndsAt=number, BatteryQuantity=integer}\n{Kind=\"ScrapExchange\", Count=integer, Credits=integer, ScrapQuantity=integer}\n{Kind=\"UpgradeBuy\", UpgradeId=\"harvest\"|\"weapon\"|\"storage\",\n Tier=1, Credits=integer}\n```\n\nEvery `ActionResult` has exact base shape\n`{RequestId, Success, Code, Revision, Payload}` where Revision is the current\nViewRevision. A duplicate whose result remains in cache returns that exact prior\nresult without rate consumption or mutation. Cache: up to 64 results/player for\n30 seconds. If an observed RequestId remains inside that 30-second replay window\nafter its result has been evicted from the bounded cache, it returns failed\n`REPLAYED`; it never re-dispatches, mutates a gameplay domain, or reinserts the\nevicted result.\n\nDelta is exactly one of:\n\n```text\n{Protocol=1, BaseRevision, Revision, ServerTime, Domain=\"Player\", Value=PlayerView}\n{Protocol=1, BaseRevision, Revision, ServerTime, Domain=\"Session\", Value=SessionView}\n{Protocol=1, BaseRevision, Revision, ServerTime, Domain=\"World\", Value=WorldView}\n```\n\nRevision mismatch makes the client discard the delta and request a snapshot.\nCountdown is interpolated from server timestamps; no per-second remote.\n\n| Channel | Burst | Refill/sec |\n|---|---:|---:|\n| GetSnapshot | 2 | 0.5 |\n| ToolIntent | 12 | 6 |\n| InteractionIntent | 6 | 3 |\n| InteractionCloseIntent | 6 | 3 |\n| UseItemIntent | 3 | 1 |\n\nGameplay cooldowns also apply. The server derives rays, validates equipped\nruntime ownership/range/LOS, and owns zombie assemblies. Clients never send\ndamage, origin, reward, price, growth, health, phase, or power values.\n\nStable result codes:\n\n```text\nOK, INVALID_PAYLOAD, UNKNOWN_REQUEST, RATE_LIMITED, REPLAYED,\nPROFILE_NOT_READY, PROFILE_LOCKED, PROFILE_LOAD_FAILED,\nPROFILE_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED, PROFILE_CONFLICT, SERVER_FULL,\nPLAYER_DEAD, NO_CHARACTER, INVALID_SLOT, NOT_EQUIPPED, COOLDOWN,\nNO_AMMO, INVALID_AIM, OUT_OF_RANGE, LINE_OF_SIGHT_BLOCKED,\nUNKNOWN_TARGET, TARGET_INACTIVE, INVENTORY_FULL, NOT_ENOUGH_ITEMS,\nNOT_ENOUGH_CREDITS, STORAGE_FULL, ALREADY_OWNED, REQUIRES_POWER,\nINVALID_TOKEN, TOKEN_EXPIRED, WRONG_INTERACTION, PLOT_OCCUPIED,\nPLOT_NOT_READY, NOT_PLOT_OWNER, PROCESSOR_BUSY, POWER_ALREADY_ACTIVE,\nKILL_REWARD_CAPPED\n```\n\n## 7. Spawn and Player Lifecycle\n\nAll spawns are `(5,1,5)`, invisible, anchored, noncolliding, neutral, Enabled,\nDuration 0, and face the bedroom exit.\n\n| SpawnId | Slot | Position |\n|---|---:|---:|\n| `bedroom_01` | 1 | `(-49,54.5,72)` |\n| `bedroom_02` | 2 | `(-41,54.5,72)` |\n| `bedroom_03` | 3 | `(-33,54.5,72)` |\n| `bedroom_04` | 4 | `(-49,54.5,62)` |\n| `bedroom_05` | 5 | `(-41,54.5,62)` |\n| `bedroom_06` | 6 | `(-33,54.5,62)` |\n\nSmallest-free slot is held for the session and reused on respawn. Lifecycle State Machines\n\nEvery delayed callback captures a lifecycle generation and returns if stale.\n\n| Trigger | Required transition / cleanup |\n|---|---|\n| startup failure | reject joins; reverse-stop; destroy Runtime; load no characters |\n| character death | invalidate tokens/tools; unregister actor; mark night death; one generation-checked respawn |\n| respawn | same slot; register actor; reprovision Tools; republish Player view |\n| player removal | exact seven-step teardown in section 7 |\n| dawn | stop spawns; invalidate wave; destroy enemies; settle survivors; enemy count zero |\n| wave completion | publish Complete; clear actor/path/connections; no second wave that night |\n| power expiry | compare generation; disable exact powered set; publish once |\n| node respawn | compare cycle; restore health/visibility/prompt once |\n| processor finish | compare job; commit reserved output; clear once |\n| BindToClose | reject requests; settle/refund; reverse-stop; save/release within 25 seconds |\n\nProcessor output capacity is reserved at job start, so successful start guarantees\ncompletion; otherwise it rejects before removing Crops.\n\n## 10. It is **not** a\nfinished-art reference. Production must reproduce its spatial logic, not copy\nits present vegetation, lighting, materials, path finish, or architectural\ndetail level.\n\n## 1. Golden Spatial Rules\n\nThese rules are mandatory for production Gate 1.\n\n1. **One coherent landform.** The approach, shoulders, bunker burial, and rear\n   ridge must read as connected geology—not separate mounds or assembled ramps.\n2. **Terrain-driven dogleg.** The primary route must make a meaningful lateral\n   displacement and heading change because of the landform.\n3. **Delayed visibility.** The bunker entrance must not be clearly readable\n   from the initial approach or immediately before the dogleg.\n4. **Reveal beat.** After the direction change, the entrance becomes the\n   primary read; the route is secondary and the enclosing ridge is tertiary.\n5. **Buried facility.** The bunker reads as a fortified opening cut into a\n   mountain. Most roof and side-shell mass remains concealed.\n6. **Direct terrain contact.** Earth meets retaining structure, portal frame,\n   and exposed construction edges without a visible gap or a dirt canopy placed\n   over a freestanding box.\n7. **Player-scale traversal.** Route width, grade, camera clearance, entrance\n   clearance, and turning space must work at Roblox avatar scale.\n8. **Asymmetry.** One side supplies stronger terrain/structural framing while\n   the other supplies counter-space. Do not mirror the reveal.\n9. **Depth layers.** Player views must contain intentional foreground framing,\n   a midground entrance/reveal, and a background ridge or landform continuation.\n10. **Terrain dominance.** Even at the reveal, the hillside remains the largest\n    visual mass. Architecture strengthens the discovery without exposing the\n    complete bunker shell.\n\n## 2. Future Visual Quality Targets\n\nEvery later gate must substantially exceed v36. FOREST\\012============================================================\\012\\012Use approved tree families first.\\012\\012The forest must not look like evenly spaced procedural poles.\\012\\012Use edge density.\\012\\012Do not use absurd random scaling.\\012\\012Preserve player navigation.\\012\\012Do not block:\\012\\012- spawn;\\012- bunker doors;\\012- farming areas;\\012- interaction anchors;\\012- major traversal corridors.\\012\\012Where existing trees have foliage:\\012\\012- foliage should generally not physically block the player unless intentional;\\012- trunks may
